IMAD Record Info

Note that some field names in IMAD records contain unprintable characters. These have been represented as control characters matching the letter they're paired with. For example, the PIAD field is followed by a ^PIAD field, where ^P corresponds to the character code 0x10. @IAD is paired with ^@IAD, with the corresponding character code being 0x00.
